Vans sequin V18–V37: the plan

20 music-only videos, 16 s each, 5 shots of 3.2 s. Three from each of Grace's 6 links (same beats, new look), plus V36 and V37 that mix the best beats. Every video has its own setting angle, outfit, shot order and grade. No voice, no on-screen text.

Total on Kie

  • 100 clips (Seedance 2.0 Mini, 4 s) $16.40
  • Redo allowance $4.60, each redo asked first; the job never passes $30.00
  • Music: free tracks, $0
  • November: $39.90 so far, at most $69.90 after this job

Order: all 100 stills → storyboard here → one example video per link (V18, V21, V24, V27, V30, V33; test clip V30S1 first, $4.92 for the 30 clips) → your OK → the other 14 videos. Every hand and ankle uses Grace's real complexion, taken from her own Vans post.

Storyboard: all 100 stills

Five shots per video, left to right. Red outline = needs a redo (25 stills, $2.25, not run yet; 2 added for arm and leg positions). Grace's complexion comes from her own Vans post: every hand and ankle should match it. Examples get made first: V18, V21, V24, V27, V30, V33.

V18S1 · hand lifts the held shoe over her legs, side tab

Still (5 refs: product_sheet.png, real_vans_tab.jpg, f_11.5.jpg, f_23.5.jpg, street.jpg)

Image 1 = the real shoe from every side (product sheet: pair, side, top with red insole, gum waffle sole, back, sequin close-up). Image 2 = the real woven side tab: a small BLACK tab with white VANS letters, sewn flat on the outer side. Image 3 = Grace's real hand holding the shoe: her exact medium-deep brown skin tone, nails, rings and bracelets (ignore the street). Image 4 = Grace's real ankles in the shoes: her exact medium-deep brown skin tone and thin gold anklet (ignore the pavement and her pants). Image 5 = the real place. VIEW AND FRAMING FIRST: Seated on the stone sidewalk: view from above of her legs stretched out on the pavement, her left foot in its shoe at the top-left; her right hand holds the RIGHT shoe above her shins in side view, the toe pointing to the TOP-LEFT corner, the black side tab facing the camera. Her LEFT foot wears the LEFT shoe, fully inside it; her RIGHT hand holds the RIGHT shoe of the pair, empty; these are the only two shoes in the scene. THE SHOE must match the product sheet and every close-up exactly, letter for letter and colour for colour: the real Vans Premium Authentic low-top lace-up sneaker in sequin checkerboard. A black and off-white checkerboard where ONLY the black squares are covered in raised black sequins and the off-white squares are plain woven canvas; black leather binding round the collar with white stitching; a black tongue; natural tan canvas lining; flat black cotton laces through four pairs of black eyelets; a small BLACK woven tab with white VANS letters on the outer side; a vertical black heel stripe; a chunky smooth off-white rubber sidewall with a small WHITE 'OFF THE WALL' label at the back; a deep RED insole with a WHITE rectangle holding the red VANS logo; a gum-brown waffle sole. The toe box is the same checkerboard as the rest of the upper, never a solid black toe or panel: every black square is its own square with off-white canvas squares between, the sequins never merge into a solid black area at the toe, heel or sides. The sidewall is OFF-WHITE all the way round; gum brown shows ONLY on the bottom tread, never on the side. The laces are BLACK. True size: an adult women's sneaker about 25 cm long, exactly the length of her own foot. No other logos, no added marks, no extra text. HER HAND is Grace's real hand, exactly like the hand reference: the same medium-deep brown skin tone, slim fingers, medium-length pink nails with white French tips, a thin blue silicone RING on one finger (a ring, never a wristband: nothing blue on her wrist), a silver heart ring, a chunky gold watch-style link bracelet and thin gold chain bracelets. Never lighter or darker skin than the reference. A cream chunky-knit sweater cuff at the wrist. The hand enters from the frame edge, whole wrist visible, five fingers, and it GRIPS the shoe: thumb on one side, fingers curled round the other. ONE BODY, NATURAL POSE: her legs, feet, arm and hand all belong to one person whose body is just behind the camera (we see from her own eyes). Every limb enters the frame from the bottom edge or from the side nearest her body: the holding forearm runs back toward that same body, and her right hand is on the same side of the frame as her right leg. No arm or hand comes in from the opposite edge or from the top of the frame; joints bend the natural way. HER LEGS: light-wash skinny jeans with ripped knees, cropped at the ankle; GRACE'S SKIN on every ankle, foot and hand: the exact medium-deep brown tone of the skin reference, never lighter or darker; a thin gold anklet. PLACE: a stone-paved sidewalk outside a cafe with small round tables, slatted chairs in pale blue and honey wood, potted plants and a chalkboard (like the place photo), close and low, the cafe chairs at the left. LIGHT: bright soft daylight, neutral white balance: the off-white squares stay off-white, no yellow, orange or golden cast, no dark or moody grade. The sequins look like real sequins in daylight, nothing more: no added sparkles, glints or light streaks. A real phone photo, casual and realistic. Vertical 9:16 photo. No text, no captions, no watermark anywhere.

Clip (Seedance 2.0 Mini, 4 s, first frame = the approved still)

her hand keeps the same grip on the heel collar and lifts the shoe slowly about 5 cm toward the lens; the shoe never turns or flips. Camera: handheld, very slight drift. Toe direction stays exactly as in the first frame: toe pointing to the TOP-LEFT corner. The shoe keeps exactly the look of the first frame the whole clip: checkerboard with sequins only on the black squares, the black side tab with white VANS letters; it never turns, flips or changes shape. Her hand keeps the same grip the whole clip, five fingers, whole wrist; the blue band stays a ring on her finger. Her arm and legs stay joined to one natural body, the hand on the same side as her leg, as in the first frame. Her skin stays the same medium-deep brown tone as the first frame. Bright neutral daylight, realistic phone footage, 9:16. No sparkle effects, no text, no captions, no music, no voice.
